[task] Make running tasks synchronously in RunOrPostTask an opt-in

RunOrPostTask() runs a task synchronously when WorkTracker believes the
bound thread is idle. A nested RunLoop inside a task is fine, because
SequenceManagerImpl::OnIdle() doesn't signal idleness while a task runs
[1]. But a nested RunLoop inside a MessagePump work item (e.g. a Windows
message handler) signals idleness, allowing sync work until the next
OnBeginWork(). If the bound thread calls RunOrPostTask() in that window,
the task runs synchronously on it and
AssociatedThreadId::IsBoundToCurrentThread() CHECKs [2].

This CL makes running tasks synchronously an opt-in
(SequenceManager::Settings::can_run_tasks_synchronously, disabled by
default). When enabled, a nested RunLoop must run inside a task (CHECK).

[1] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:base/task/sequence_manager/sequence_manager_impl.cc;l=672-687;drc=f7c3b6db91ae631335c81d7777d5ac45efa217e0
[2] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:base/task/sequence_manager/associated_thread_id.cc;l=41-45;drc=6ec303413adbe995e7e4e43218fcb4ac9dee7e30

still needs to approve resource changes for their own widget. A per-file
entry now adds me as an owner of res/, to make reviews easier as I add
new widgets for ttc.

Simplify Mojo vector copies and span usage in page-state serialization

- Assign frame->document_state and page->referenced_files directly in
  WriteMojoFrameState and WriteMojoPageState instead of copying elements
  in a loop (retaining the non-nullopt check via std::ranges::all_of).
- Pass the byte vector directly to mojom::Element::NewBytes in
  WriteResourceRequestBody instead of calling base::ToVector.
- Use the base::span overload of mojom::PageState::Deserialize in
  ReadMojoPageState.

[omnibox] Set omnibox input alignment based on UI LTR/RTL direction

The Views omnibox uses UI direction to decide how to align text in the
input text area. The full webui omnibox was using `auto`, which could
switch based on what the user types. That was never correct and
would cause the input location to jump and differ from the rest of the
UI. With this change, the full webui omnibox works as the Views
implementation did, keeping input text and the rest of the UI
consistently aligned.

c4c0950cf8e273f6e6f57dc5df1e31dca12fe5c9 [ diff ] 
[Vertical Tabs] Key DropTargetResult destination group by Token

DropTargetResult previously stored a representative destGroupTabId and
TabGroupRange resolved groupTabs.get(0).getId(), forcing
VerticalTabListCoordinator to look up the Tab again on drop to recover
its group Token.

Storing @Nullable Token destGroupId directly on DropTargetResult removes
the representative tab dependency across drop target calculation,
indicator indentation, and cross-window drop execution, falling back to
a standalone drop if the target group is empty at drop time.

WebMCP: Make ModelContextHost navigation-associated
📋 Metadata
commit
c4c0950cf8e273f6e6f57dc5df1e31dca12fe5c9

tree
d0d5db7242f6f60751d18e5729f317dcfcdefbd7
parent
97e0e7a118540316fe0e1333da86f5a3746951eb [ diff ] 
WebMCP: Make ModelContextHost navigation-associated

ModelContextHost messages are currently not ordered with navigation
messages. This is a problem to detect tool stability for the following
reason:

1. The actor stack uses the navigation lifecycle (loading state) to
   decide when the page is stable and its tools should be read.
2. The tools themselves are registered in the browser via the
   ModelContextHost interface.

If messages from 1) and 2) are not ordered, tool registration can
arrive after a navigation lifecycle update and the consumer can read
incomplete tool state. This change fixes this by making
ModelContextHost a navigation-associated interface.

ModelContext, which the renderer passes to the browser via
ModelContextHost, becomes associated with it too. Otherwise, messages
the browser sends on ModelContext race with replies on
ModelContextHost. For example, RegisterScriptTool() sends
NotifyToolChange() to the registering Document on ModelContext and
then replies on ModelContextHost. On separate pipes, the order of the
`toolchange` event and the registerTool() promise resolution was
nondeterministic. Now `toolchange` always fires first.

The change brought up a design question about prerendering. Currently
messages on ModelContextHost are buffered for prerendered Documents.
Making it navigation-associated requires strict ordering with
navigation IPCs, so its messages can no longer be buffered (since
navigation IPCs have to be processed for prerendered pages). We grant
access to ModelContextHost to prerendered pages since the current set
of APIs don't have any user visible side-effects. If that changes, the
API implementation will need to define the behaviour for prerendered
pages.

parent
b408c16516a5d1281b58ecc2a755d6991faf3553 [ diff ] 
[Vertical Tabs] Merge external drops into existing group by Token

When a tab dragged from another window is dropped onto a group in the
vertical tab rail, VerticalTabListCoordinator already resolves the
destination group Token to compute the insertion index, then looks up
the destination Tab a second time after the move to call
mergeListOfTabsToGroup.

Passing the resolved Token directly to mergeListOfTabsToExistingGroup
avoids the extra tab lookup and removes the dependency on a
representative Tab when merging into an existing group.

[AIM] Populate Fusebox attachment labels from InputState

Hardcoding client strings for the Camera, Gallery, Files, and Drive
attachment buttons at view inflation time prevented server-configured
menu labels from taking effect and forced view code to branch on desktop
platform state. Binding resolved labels from the session input state
lets server configs drive attachment menu labels while recording client
fallback usage per input source so fallbacks can eventually be removed.

b0168ea81fd1bc75531fff47e6807838990b8101 [ diff ] 
[OnDeviceModel] Wait for initial asset loading

Previously, ManifestBrokerState ran EnsureInitialization() callbacks as
soon as ManifestMonitor selected a manifest, while
ManifestSolutionFactory's SolutionProviders were still uninitialized,
transiently sending ModelSubscriber::Unavailable() with kUnknown. Queue
EnsureInitialization() tasks until ManifestSolutionFactory finishes
loading initial asset states and solution configs via
OnSolutionFactoryInit(), bind
ManifestSolutionFactory::OnAssetStateUpdated() via WeakPtr so a replaced
factory cannot invoke OnSolutionFactoryInit() after destruction, and
always post AssetManager::InstallerRegistered() in
OverrideManifestAssetManagerDelegate::RegisterOnDemandComponent() so
uninstalled overrides do not stall initialization.

Extract BrokerInit (BrokerInit::TaskCallback, QueueTask(),
is_complete(), and Complete()) to share deferred broker initialization
across ManifestBrokerState and ModelBrokerAndroid. Complete() runs
queued tasks on initial completion and updates capabilities_ on
subsequent calls.

Remove ScopedModelBrokerFeatureList from FakeManifestBroker and declare
it before TaskEnvironment in test callers. Previously, tests declaring
TaskEnvironment before FakeManifestBroker initialized and reset
ScopedFeatureList while ThreadPool worker threads were starting in
ThreadFunc() and calling FeatureList::IsEnabled() outside
TestTaskTracker's execution fence.

[UI] Migrate ui/ to `ui::mojom::WindowOpenDisposition`

Spell the type as `ui::mojom::WindowOpenDisposition` instead of the
deprecated `WindowOpenDisposition` alias, and include
"ui/base/mojom/window_open_disposition.mojom-shared.h" instead of
"ui/base/window_open_disposition.h". Remove includes of the old header
that nothing uses.

Headers that only use the type in declarations include the mojom
forward header instead.

This CL covers:
  ui/

No behavior change.

This CL was generated by running the following from src/, then
`git cl format`:

  migrate_window_open_disposition.py --migrate-type ui
  check_alias_includes.py --enumerators ui

By hand, partly as asked in review: add "//ui/base/mojom" to the
public_deps of //ui/views/controls/webview and //ui/web_dialogs, whose
headers now include the mojom forward header (for `gn check`); use
#include for the mojom header in .mm files; name the enumerator the
mojom way (`kNewForegroundTab`) and use backticks for code names in the
touched comments; add braces to conditionals in
window_open_disposition_utils.cc; and address clang-tidy findings in
ui/web_dialogs/test/test_web_contents_handler.{h,cc}: concatenate the
nested namespaces and use `= default` for the trivial constructor and
destructor.

The scripts are attached to https://crbug.com/373551504.

[Actor] Gate live notifications on Android 16+

Requires Android 16 or higher for live ongoing notifications so
promoted ongoing extras, live task preemption, and the 30-second
ongoing window for completed notifications only apply on Android 16+.
On Android 15 and below, completed notifications are immediately
dismissible without scheduling a demotion delay.

extending ChromeTabbedActivityTestRule, remove its pass-through

BaseCtaTransitTestRule directly.

  BaseCtaTransitTestRule
- Pass ChromeActivity explicitly into RuntimePermissionTestUtils, and
  drop PermissionTestRule from SiteSettingsCookiesTest since it never
  triggers permission prompts.

tree
78b6c6af41ebf879d4a6b57e2fde75358a1504d0
parent
e6160cd83cbd602cc8aedd093e7dea6f8583c7ba [ diff ] 
[iOS] Pass initiator origin to ImageDataFetcher::FetchImageData()

Replace the boolean send_cookies parameter on
ImageDataFetcher::FetchImageData() with an optional initiator
url::Origin. When an initiator is provided, configure the
ResourceRequest with request_initiator, CredentialsMode::kSameOrigin,
and SiteForCookies::FromOrigin(*initiator).

This ensures that URLLoader re-evaluates same-origin credential
eligibility on every redirect hop (stripping cookies on cross-origin
redirects) and populates accurate Sec-Fetch-Site headers instead of
sending Sec-Fetch-Site: none.

[Pinning V2] Hide the ephemeral toolbar button when ineligible

Under Pinning V2,
ContextualTasksSidePanelCoordinator::OnEligibilityChange closes the side
panel when entry point eligibility is lost (e.g. the default search
engine changes), but ContextualTasksEphemeralButtonController kept the
task's tabs marked eligible, so a thread-state toolbar button lingered
until navigation, the auto-dismiss timer, or tab close.

Subscribe the controller to
EntryPointEligibilityManager::RegisterOnEntryPointEligibilityChanged
and, on losing eligibility, clear all tab eligibility and auto-dismiss
timers and record the button's shown duration as TaskDisassociated.
Tasks are not disassociated, so a pinned icon still resumes the thread
if the user becomes eligible again. This applies to both the legacy
ContextualTasksButton and the kContextualTasksEphemeralPinButtonRefactor
pinned action.

The pre-V2 ephemeral entry point intentionally stays visible when AIM
eligibility is lost (see ButtonVisibilityNotTiedToAimEligibility), so
the new behavior is gated on kContextualTasksPinningV2.

Reland "[Unified Tabstrip] Add trailing padding for collapsed group header"

This is a reland of commit 37f6550f4365f6fdabc063007675a000dcb3d047

Replaced base::test::RunUntil in HorizontalTabGroupViewBrowserTest::ToggleCollapse
with synchronous EXPECT_EQ(group_view->IsCollapsed(), expected_collapsed).
Pumping the message loop until idle in RunUntil allowed queued
WindowEventDispatcher::SynthesizeMouseMoveEvent and views::MouseWatcher
(300ms timer) tasks to exit tab closing mode on slower builders
(Linux ASan LSan and linux-arm64-dbg) while renderer processes were starting.

Original change's description:
> [Unified Tabstrip] Add trailing padding for collapsed group header
>
> When a collapsed tab group is the last item in the horizontal tab strip,
> its header chip ends flush with the container edge, leaving 0px padding
> before the New Tab Button (whereas an expanded group ends with a tab
> whose bottom extension provides trailing space).
>
> Add trailing padding (GetTrailingGroupHeaderPadding()) before the New
> Tab Button when the last visible child in
> UnpinnedTabContainerViewHorizontalLayout is a collapsed TabGroupView,
> and account for the trailing vs. non-trailing spacing delta when locking
> tab widths on mouse/touch group collapse.
>
> Bug: 574631886

parent
92d56fb07c07abf3e6c1b13ab48714a5271c75ef [ diff ] 
[Views] Crash if MenuModelAdapter is destroyed during BuildMenu()

Rebuilding a showing menu fires accessibility events synchronously. If a
caller owns MenuModelAdapter directly (like ToolkitDelegateViews) and is
destroyed during that callback, BuildMenu() resumes on freed memory.

CHECK(!is_building_) in ~MenuModelAdapter() to crash before that
happens.

parent
e5ad7fa3951cbda700f1e51c52e93c45c35a81b6 [ diff ] 
[NTP] Gate unsupported <ntp-app> features in NTP component extension

Prepares the NTP component extension's loadTimeData and Mojo frame
binders for mounting <ntp-app> directly from //c/b/r/new_tab_page:

- NewTabPageExtensionConfigProvider::GetLoadTimeData() explicitly sets
  loadTimeData flags for NTP subsystems not yet supported in the
  extension (OneGoogleBar, shortcuts, modules, Microsoft auth module,
  threads rail, browser promos, Customize Chrome button, and wallpaper
  search button) to disabled, and provides default values for the
  $i18n{backgroundColor} and $i18n{backgroundImageUrl} placeholders in
  new_tab_page.html.
- NewTabPageExtensionBinderProvider registers no-op binders for the
  Mojo interfaces that <ntp-app> binds unconditionally during startup
  (CustomizeButtonsHandlerFactory, HelpBubbleHandlerFactory, and
  TrackedElementHandler) so their WebUI-only default binders in
  PopulateChromeWebUIFrameBinders do not terminate the extension
  renderer before those features are enabled.

b808c828407317d61a1fe74c62c963a352b0c755 [ diff ] 
[WebApps] Move Manifest and WebAppBannerData inline optionals to heap

InstallablePageData::ManifestProperty, MLInstallabilityPromoter, and
AppBannerManager previously held std::optional<blink::Manifest> (2,232
bytes) and std::optional<WebAppBannerData> (2,680 bytes) inline across
every tab, keeping roughly 7 KiB of storage reserved even when a page
has no web app manifest or installability check is inactive.

Replace these inline std::optional members with std::unique_ptr and
reorder InstallablePageData::ManifestProperty fields to eliminate
interior padding, allocating manifest and banner data only when fetched.

tree
a67eda6c69f52a07bee2a14fba100734820b6f47
parent
e750ef21c43b60ec56b869476fb5abecc7d17881 [ diff ] 
[protobuf] Drop obsolete 0010-uninline-generated-code.patch

`0010-uninline-generated-code.patch` marks generated `Swap()` methods
`PROTOBUF_NOINLINE` in `src/google/protobuf/compiler/cpp/message.cc`.
When the patch was added in 2016 (crrev.com/c/1842653006), it un-inlined
both `MergeFromFail()` and `Swap()` in `.pb.cc`, which generated move
operations called.

Since then, upstream removed `MergeFromFail()`, changed generated move
assignment and `UnsafeArenaSwap()` to call `InternalSwap()` directly
(leaving no generated callers of `Swap()` besides the `friend void swap`
ADL wrapper), and reduced `Swap()` in `.pb.h` to a 5-line arena check
that delegates to out-of-line `InternalSwap()` or `GenericSwap()`.

Why this is safe to drop outside of a roll: On protobuf v36.0, generated
code already uses `InternalSwap()`, and keeping `PROTOBUF_NOINLINE` on
the 5-line `.pb.h` wrapper no longer saves binary size
(`android-binary-size` is -200 bytes without it). Dropping it now also
avoids a patch replay conflict on `message.cc` in the next roll.

Drop `0010-uninline-generated-code.patch`, revert `Swap()` in
`message.cc` to `inline` to match upstream, remove the patch entry from
`README.chromium`, and regenerate the checked-in bootstrap C++ headers
(`descriptor.pb.h` and `compiler/plugin.pb.h`) with the updated
`protoc`.

WebUIToolbar: Fix TutorialInteractiveUitest.SampleTutorial

When WebUIToolbar is enabled, kToolbarAppMenuButtonElementId is tracked
as a ui::TrackedElementWebUI rather than a views::TrackedElementViews.
TutorialInteractiveUitest.SampleTutorial previously registered only
views::test::InteractionTestUtilSimulatorViews in its local
ui::test::InteractionTestUtil, causing PressButton() on
kToolbarAppMenuButtonElementId to return ActionResult::kNotAttempted.
Because the button was never activated, the tutorial did not advance to
its final bubble step, leaving GetDefaultButtonForTesting() null on the
non-terminal bubble and crashing when pressing the default button.

Wait for the initial WebUI toolbar in SetUpOnMainThread(), populate
browser and WebUI interaction test simulators via
InteractionTestUtilBrowser::PopulateSimulators(), and wait for the
final help bubble with a default button to be displayed before pressing
it.

ee05492a3f928a31c61d5586633d11499df49eaf
parent
0554b6f56a3b0923a28ce1e54472aeedddfd10fe [ diff ] 
[Win]: Avoid creating console windows in subprocesses with --enable-logging

When Chrome is launched on Windows with --enable-logging (such as
--enable-logging=stderr under Telemetry), RunContentProcess calls
base::RouteStdioToConsole(create_console=true) before
CommonSubprocessInit calls base::RouteStdioToConsole(false) [C]. For
unsandboxed utility processes such as
video_capture.mojom.VideoCaptureService where
AttachConsole(ATTACH_PARENT_PROCESS) fails, RouteStdioToConsole(true)
invokes AllocConsole() and pops a foreground Win32 console window on top
of the browser window, preventing DWM from promoting DirectComposition
video swap chains to hardware overlays [C, E].

Only allow RunContentProcess to create a new console when
!IsSubprocess() [C].

Bug: 578512558, 567498386
Test: trace_test OverlayModeTraceTest_DirectComposition_Video_MP4_FourColors_Aspect_4x3

c0229858b630f18add749ea3b24491881c1a4600 [ diff ] 
[protobuf] Drop obsolete 0004-fix-shared-library-exports.patch

`0004-fix-shared-library-exports.patch` gates the legacy
`LIBPROTOBUF_EXPORT` and `LIBPROTOC_EXPORT` macros in
`src/google/protobuf/stubs/port.h` on `COMPONENT_BUILD` and adds
`__attribute__((visibility("default")))` for non-MSVC component builds.

Upstream protobuf has long since replaced those legacy macros with
`PROTOBUF_EXPORT` and `PROTOC_EXPORT` in `port_def.inc`, which already
emit `__attribute__((visibility("default")))` when `PROTOBUF_USE_DLLS`
and `LIBPROTOBUF_EXPORTS` or `LIBPROTOC_EXPORTS` are defined.

Why this is safe to drop outside of a roll: Neither `LIBPROTOBUF_EXPORT`
nor `LIBPROTOC_EXPORT` has any callers in Chromium or in protobuf v36.0,
so reverting `stubs/port.h` to stock v36.0 is a no-op for the build and
shrinks the patch queue ahead of the next roll.

Drop `0004-fix-shared-library-exports.patch`, restore
`src/google/protobuf/stubs/port.h` to its upstream state, and remove the
patch entry from `README.chromium`.

[webauthn] Fix flaky CmtgKeyTimeout test

GPM timers used the injected tick clock but posted to the real task
runner, so test mock time had no effect. In CmtgKeyTimeout, the CMTG
timeout could fire before the transaction waited for keys, skipping
the BlockedDelay histogram.

Post GPM timers to the injected task runner and have the test wait
until the transaction is blocked before fast-forwarding.

glic: Update floating prompt chip ordering and styling

When the floating prompt is positioned above a selection, reverse the
container and chip row layout direction so the searchbox remains closest
to the selection and suggestions flow upward, keeping the first
suggestion closest to the input box.

Additionally, match the action chip row width to the searchbox, cap chip
widths, and truncate suggestion labels with an ellipsis after two lines.
Unit and interactive UI tests are added to verify the ordering and
styling behavior.

tree
f255916fab2d4605c55d7a8a9246fa4406ffb7f1
parent
3af87e4eaef8d554983cb8a61be86eab4459a760 [ diff ] 
Consolidate PKIMetadata component processing and IPCs

Previously it made separate background tasks to read each file, and
sent separate mojom calls to the CertVerifierServiceFactory for each
update type. This would result in wastefully doing multiple
CertVerifierService updates (and thus flushing verifier caches multiple
times, etc) in short succession. The design would also make it more
messy to track when the component loading as a whole has completed
processing, which a later CL will want to do.

Refactor so that all files are read in a single background task, then
processed and sent to the CertVerifierServiceFactory in a single mojo
call.

Adds a network_service_only mode so that redundant updates are not sent
to the CertVerifierServiceFactory in cases where only the network
service data needs to be reloaded.

parent
4bc632656c7b95bc4c1c8c6a891e63396bdd0d52 [ diff ] 
Fix style invalidation for moveBefore into shadow root and/or slot

This fixes four distinct under-invalidation bugs when an element is
inserted into a ShadowRoot or slot element via state-preserving move. In
all cases, this results in the Element's LayoutObject surviving the move
and continuing to paint in its previous location.

1. When an element is moved outside the flat tree, style invalidation is
skipped because it's assumed that a later call to
SlotAssignment::RecalcAssignment will do the necessary invalidations for
elements removed from the flat tree. However, this logic missed two
corner cases:

  - If an element is inserted into a shadow host that doesn't have
    a slot assignment, RecalcAssignment() will be a no-op and the
    necessary invaldation of unslotted elements doesn't happen.
    Fixed in Node::ShouldSkipMarkingStyleDirty().

  - If an unslotted element is moved into a <slot> that does manual
    slotting, during RecalcAssignment it will only invalidate unslotted
    nodes that were previously slotted, which misses the new node
    because it has never been slotted. Fixed in
    SlotAssignment::RecalcAssignment().

2. In ContainerNode::ChildrenChanged, FlatTreeParentChanged() is called
on an inserted element only if `this` is an Element. This misses the
case of adding a node to the ShadowRoot itself. Fixed by checking
ParentOrShadowHostElement() of the inserted node.

3. An element is movedBefore'd into a <slot> with no assigned nodes.
Because the slot has no assigned nodes, GetStyleRecalcParent(), which is
based on stale slot assignments, treats the element as active fallback
content, and style invalidation is skipped, since it is assumed that
FlatTreeParentChanged() will take care of that. If another element is
then inserted into the shadow host without an intervening slot recalc,
the new slot child becomes inactive fallback on the next slot recalc,
but RemovedFromFlatTree() is never called on the new slot child because
RemovedFromFlatTree() is only called on the slot's prior
flat_tree_children_. Fixed in HTMLSlotElement::RecalcFlatTreeChildren().

Fixed: 576136338,577332970,575200203

[Omnibox Everywhere] Implement Taskbar Pinning on Windows

This CL adds the implementation for
`OmniboxEverywhereController::OfferPinToTaskbar`, which is used to show
a prompt asking users to pin Omnibox Everywhere to the Windows taskbar.

This method is not called from any production code yet, but can be
tested using the internal debug webui.

parent
7f59b4630b8fb1a53a8ebda5bc739a01c4f38bd6 [ diff ] 
WebUIToolbar: Fix WebUIHelpBubbleTakesFocus

With WebUIToolbar enabled, the app menu button is hosted in the WebUI
toolbar (registered as a TrackedElementWebUI for
kToolbarAppMenuButtonElementId) rather than a Views View, and focusing
it via ToolbarView::SetPaneFocusAndFocusAppMenu() completes
asynchronously over Mojo. As a result, checking views::View::HasFocus on
kToolbarAppMenuButtonElementId in
BrowserUserEducationServiceUiTest.WebUIHelpBubbleTakesFocus failed
because the element is not a View.

Update FocusAppMenuButton() in BrowserUserEducationServiceUiTest to poll
ToolbarView::GetAppMenuFocused() via PollViewProperty and WaitForState,
which works for both the Views and WebUI app menu implementations.

tree
ee3b479fd9ac567ce775dc48a1cc42a3b6595588
parent
d82d4674082500268bc8899fb21254a5797ed982 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Prompt to open App Store in Incognito for iTunes URLs

Previously, ITunesUrlsHandlerTabHelper ignored iTunes/App Store product
URLs in off-the-record (Incognito) mode because
SKStoreProductViewController loads the user's Apple ID account in-app.
As a result, navigating to an App Store URL in Incognito allowed the web
navigation to proceed instead of offering to open the App Store.

With this change, when a visible off-the-record WebState navigates to an
iTunes product URL in the main frame:
- Cancel the web navigation and queue an AppLaunchConfirmationRequest
  overlay (kOpenAppStoreFromIncognito) in the web content area queue.
- Delay the latest ShouldAllowRequest() or ShouldAllowResponse() policy
  decision callback while the prompt is active (canceling any superseded
  pending callback) so transient tabs do not close early and in-flight
  navigations do not commit and dismiss the overlay.
- If accepted, upgrade http:// to https:// and open the URL externally
  with UIApplicationOpenURLOptionUniversalLinksOnly, showing a
  kAppLaunchFailed alert at the front of the queue if launching fails.
- Record Tab.ExternalApplicationOpened.AppStoreFromIncognito and
  Tab.ExternalApplicationOpened.Failed metrics, and update
  DeeplinkSigninTestCase/testCrossDeviceSigninInIncognito for the new
  prompt string.

[DomDistiller] Fail distillation on page handle URL mismatch

When a caller passes an unowned SourcePageHandleWebContents whose
committed main-frame URL doesn't match the target URL (e.g. after a
renderer crash/discard), DistillPageImpl previously fell back to
creating a new background WebContents. Fail distillation immediately
instead, while keeping the CreateNewWebContents path for handle-less
requests.

ash/arc: Remove chrome_paths_internal.h dependency from NearbyShareSessionImpl

chrome::GetUserCacheDirectory() returns a path other than the given
profile directory only if the profile directory is under the XDG config
directory (e.g. ~/.config), which is never the case for ChromeOS user
profiles (/home/chronos/u-<hash>). Use the profile path directly to
locate the obsolete <user_cache_dir>/.NearbyShare directory, and remove
chrome_paths_internal.h from chrome/browser/ash/arc/DEPS.

[ios] Demote iPhone iOS 26 and iPad iOS 27 on full-configs to CI

Mark the iPhone iOS 26.5 (SIM_IPHONE_16_26_5, SIM_IPHONE_16_PLUS_26_5)
and iPad iOS 27.0 (SIM_IPAD_A16_27_0) test variants in
ios_simulator_full_configs_tests as ci_only.

The shared chromium.tests Mac-26 arm64 pool is saturated, leaving
ios-simulator-full-configs try builds pending near the 2h scheduling
timeout. Demoting these variants halves try shard fan-out per build
from 226 shards (106 suites) to 113 shards (53 suites) while keeping
iPhone iOS 27.0 (SIM_IPHONE_16_27_0, SIM_IPHONE_16_PLUS_27_0) and
iPad iOS 26.5 (SIM_IPAD_PRO_7TH_GEN_26_5) coverage on the CQ and
preserving full coverage across all 312 shards on CI. Restoring CQ
coverage for the demoted variants is deferred until Mac-26 arm64 pool
capacity recovers.

Note: a CL could land a regression that only shows on iPhone iOS 26.5
or iPad iOS 27.0. The ci/ios-simulator-full-configs mirror still runs
these variants and would catch it, but that builder has no owner.


No-Try: true

6ae475b41308d42ffa28600518417a320bbdd695
parent
05599265e0ca101e2b94b612c3c3cc277ccc6dc9 [ diff ] 
[bookmarks] Improve touch drag responsiveness and selection handling

Makes drag-and-drop in the Bookmark Manager feel more reliable on
touchscreens:

- Long-press to select, then drag, no longer gets stuck. Previously,
  moving the finger right after the long-press selected the row (but
  before the drag timer fired) cancelled the drag and left the gesture
  unresponsive. Moving past touch slop now starts the drag immediately.
- Tapping a selected row (or its checkmark) to deselect it is no longer
  misread as a drag. A stationary hold on a selected row now waits the
  standard 500ms long-press delay instead of 100ms, and swiping on a
  selected row still scrolls the list.
- Touching the drag handle starts dragging instantly. Touchscreen events
  were misclassified as mouse input (SOURCE_CLASS_POINTER also matches
  touchscreens), so drags only began after the finger moved.
- Swiping on a row and lifting no longer opens the bookmark by accident.
- Removes a redundant drag handle hit-test from the row touch listener,
  since the handle's own listener already consumes those touches.

Screen recording:
http://screencast/cast/NDkwMzY3MjQ5MTI3ODMzNnxjOWI0NmZhOC0wNg

Bug: 562574156

[cleanup] Fix `MOCK_METHOD` macro in webid

Missing comma in `MOCK_METHOD(..., (const override))` silently drops
`override`, potentially hiding dead mocks.

This CL adds a comma into `(const, override)` part of `MOCK_METHOD`
macro.

tree
1bf7b1e44a9517b9e6e821bd1aad0aff5aac9890
parent
6b95e727dee67193a8a6470cb2de9306def3a20d [ diff ] 
[TTC] Offer create_window and close_window on desktop windowing

TTC didn't expose any window management tools. Now that the actor's
CreateWindow and CloseWindow work on Android, offer them where Chrome
runs in a desktop-style windowing environment with multiple user-visible
windows: desktop platforms, and Android in desktop mode. On phones a
second window means split-screen, which isn't what a user asking for a
new window expects, so the tools are neither advertised nor dispatched
there.

create_window comes from the actor ToolRegistry as-is. The new window
becomes active, and the session's tracked tab follows the active window
through VoiceFocusedContentsTracker, so subsequent tools act in the new
window without extra plumbing.

close_window is defined by TTC rather than taken from the registry: the
registry definition takes a window_id parameter, but the model has no
window IDs. TTC closes the window containing the tracked tab, the same
way enter_fullscreen and exit_fullscreen resolve their window.

activate_window is left for a follow-up since it needs a way for the
model to refer to a window.

Bug: 470475787
Test: ToolControllerBrowserTest.* and ToolControllerWindowToolsBrowserTest.* (android_browsertests on android_36 emulator, browser_tests on Linux)

[TTC] Add live recipe selection flag for TTC

Add the #ttc-live-recipe flag in chrome://flags and the kTtcLiveRecipe
feature parameter in components/ttc/ to enable selecting the Beyond Live
streaming model for TTC.

This change adds:
1. kTtcLiveRecipe feature and parameter in components/ttc/features with
   options for Fallback and Default models.
2. Registration of #ttc-live-recipe on chrome://flags with Fallback and
   Default model options.
3. model_preference wiring in TtcMesClient::Initialize and Session Journal
   logging for chrome://ttc-internals.
4. Unit tests in ttc_mes_client_unittest and assertions in
   protocol_browsertest.

This satisfies reviewer feedback from David Bokan to allow local
developer model toggling and Finch experimentation matching the
contract in cl/1010802908.

Android: Use chrome_r8 --disassemble for DEX disassembly

Update tools/binary_size/libsupersize/dex_disassembly.py and
third_party/r8/disassemble.py to run the GraalVM native-image chrome_r8
binary with --disassemble on Linux x64 rather than starting a JVM. Falls
back to the JVM elsewhere.

On many-core machines, also pass -XX:ActiveProcessorCount=32 to limit
G1 GC threads (on a 96-core machine: sys time 123ms -> 76ms, wall time
501ms -> 488ms for a 415 KB .dex.jar).

parent
18b78539db58af2bf8b59ac44103bda9b9b67713 [ diff ] 
[omnibox] Suppress DSE keyword description on iOS

On mobile, plain text search suggestions do not display the default
search engine name (e.g. "Google") as a description. Android already
suppresses this in AutocompleteController::UpdateKeywordDescriptions(),
while iOS previously relied on suppressing it downstream in
AutocompleteMatchFormatter.mm.

This CL aligns iOS with Android in AutocompleteController by omitting
the DSE description on iOS as well. This ensures search suggestions do
not carry an unused DSE keyword description in shared C++ on mobile and
updates the comment in AutocompleteMatchFormatter.mm accordingly.

Bug: None

[ntp] Make RecentTabsPagePrefs and MostVisitedSites metrics static

Eliminate native heap wrapper allocation and redundant native pointers
across NTP preference and metrics JNI boundaries:
- Convert RecentTabsPagePrefs to stateless static JNI methods taking
  @JniType("Profile*") Profile directly, removing the C++ wrapper object
  lifecycle (init/destroy).
- Remove unused native pointer parameters from static metric-recording
  JNI methods on MostVisitedSitesBridge.

[TabUI] Prune unused GN dependencies across chrome/browser

dependencies on tab_ui targets, or depended on the monolithic

[OE FRE][2/3] Clean up OmniboxEverywhereHandlerTest setup

Suppress the TestingProfile's default `OmniboxEverywhereService` factory
in `OmniboxEverywhereHandlerTest::GetTestingFactories()` so a second
`OmniboxEverywhereFreController` does not observe and mutate the same
profile/local-state prefs alongside the test fixture's `mock_service_`.

Also wrap `mock_service_` in `testing::NiceMock` and factor out a
`CreateHandlerWithPage()` helper to remove repeated handler construction
boilerplate across tests.

This CL is required for moving FRE impression recording to the WebUI
lifecycle, where follow-up `RecordFreImpression()` handler tests query
popup visibility on `mock_service_` and assert stateful FRE session
transitions without interference from a duplicate profile-owned
controller.

Android: Destroy incognito-only browser windows when they become empty
📋 Metadata
commit
cb9d9d1741083ece4b5417f4a6443468ecc53674
[ log ]
author
Calder Kitagawa <ckitagawa@chromium.org>
Tue Nov 03 17:54:06 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Tue Nov 03 17:54:06 2026
tree
15d41a631b9867cb6b42b657562307719437f472
parent
8654c3203315c77b1ec2d766ebd0670f9a24212e [ diff ] 
Android: Destroy incognito-only browser windows when they become empty

ChromeAndroidTaskImpl only observed IncognitoTabModel for
SupportedProfileType.MIXED Activities. Dedicated incognito windows
(SupportedProfileType.OFF_THE_RECORD) also use IncognitoTabModel, which
destroys its native TabListInterface as soon as it becomes empty. If
another incognito window kept the OTR Profile alive, nothing removed the
window's AndroidBrowserWindow, so it stayed enumerable with a null
TabListInterface until the Activity was asynchronously destroyed.

Observe IncognitoTabModel for OFF_THE_RECORD Activities too. These
Activities attach windowing features to the incognito Profile, so also
remove those features before destroying the window; otherwise
BrowserExtensionWindowController outlives its UnownedUserDataHost.
Before this change, adding a new tab to such an emptied window crashed
on a dangling TabListInterface in
BrowserExtensionWindowController::GetTabCount().

The new integration test was verified to fail without the
ChromeAndroidTaskImpl change (the stale incognito browser window was
still enumerated).

tree
74a9893429606c961626935f161b2ae6761cd444
parent
096545acf045311a371879b0d38a700a42d19136 [ diff ] 
[Searchbox] Restrict shared component OWNERS

During componentization onto SearchboxMixin, EXTENDED_OWNERS and
per-file rules temporarily allowed broad components/omnibox and
components/search ownership across shared searchbox subcomponents and
styles. Now that embedders use SearchboxMixin, remove EXTENDED_OWNERS
and its per-file rules so all shared files are gated by core searchbox
stewards to prevent embedder-specific logic from leaking in. Also update
top-level OWNERS to add shomizoe@ and yvonneshi@ and remove romanarora@.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
[ozone/wayland] Ignore configures that predate set_fullscreen
📋 Metadata
commit
a6698d4e0d03fbd268312dae603d2b5b0394dfc5
[ log ]
author
Vikram Pasupathy <vpasupathy@chromium.org>
Tue Nov 03 17:51:25 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Tue Nov 03 17:51:25 2026
tree
5de7e6a0c1cd672ae826615f57347cc44951d012
parent
585c3b99f31f6e9c8de776b23a783cdc506aea84 [ diff ] 
[ozone/wayland] Ignore configures that predate set_fullscreen

With kAsyncFullscreenWindowState disabled, SetFullscreen(true) puts the
window into kFullScreen right away, before the compositor replies. A
configure sent before the compositor saw set_fullscreen does not have
the fullscreen state set, so it was dropping the window back out of
fullscreen and clearing previously_maximized_. On linux-wayland-mutter
bots, this caused FullscreenWaiter enter and exit waits to time out in
browser_tests and interactive_ui_tests.

Send a wl_display.sync after set_fullscreen, the same way Minimize()
does after set_minimized. Configures that arrive while
fullscreen_sync_callback_ is set predate set_fullscreen, so keep the
window in kFullScreen and derive previously_maximized_ from the
underlying configure state. Keep the current bounds on non-maximized or
empty stale configures so a 0x0 initial configure does not leave
bounds_dip empty, while honoring the configured size on stale maximized
configures so compositors like Weston do not raise
`xdg_wm_base.invalid_surface_state`.

Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:linux-wayland-weston-rel

b882c81478ca7ec3ff19bef73c81b7065bbee2f1
parent
6d76c905421e454c95ac6987ebdc5db3cba886e5 [ diff ] 
[Android] Optimize thumbnail resize, ETC1 decompression, and JPEG write

During Hub tab switcher card expansion and live tab readback,
TabContentManager::SendThumbnailToJava previously ran a full-resolution
2-pass skia::ImageOperations::Resize convolution on CrBrowserMain even
when no downsampling was needed, ThumbnailCache bounced between disk
read and ThreadPool decompression through CrBrowserMain (alongside a
redundant PathExists stat check), and JpegThumbnailHelper copied the
compressed JPEG buffer on CrBrowserMain while compressing at BEST_EFFORT
priority.

Skip the 1:1 no-op resize fast-path (and use RESIZE_GOOD when
downsampling), chain ETC1 file read directly into ThreadPool
decompression via ReadAndDecompress without the intermediate UI-thread
hop, and std::move the JPEG buffer while matching USER_VISIBLE
compression priority (-20.7% Hub expand CrBrowserMain CPU, -33.2 ms per
expand on Pixel 9 Pro).

Fixed: 581090961

[Composebox] Show Ask Google hint in SRP mode

Previously, Composebox always defaulted its input placeholder to the AIM
hint ("Ask anything" from InputState.hintText or
searchboxComposePlaceholder), including when dynamic AIM fulfillment was
in SRP mode (willFulfillInAim === false).

Expose IDS_NTP_SEARCH_BOX_DYNAMIC_PLACEHOLDER_ASK_GOOGLE as
searchboxSrpComposePlaceholder in SearchboxHandler and use it as the
default placeholder when willFulfillInAim is false, including on initial
render before getInputState() resolves. Evaluate localized tool
fallbacks (Deep Search and Create Images) before the default placeholder
so SRP-fulfilled tools like ImageGen preserve their tool-specific hint
when ToolConfig.hintText is empty.

Manual Testing
---------------

- **will_fulfill_in_aim = true:** http://screencast/cast/NTc1MDc4MDQzMzc5MzAyNHwxNTE3MmE2Mi04OA
- **will_fulfill_in_aim = false:** http://screencast/cast/NjUzMDY5MzQxMTYzNTIwMHwyNTFlMDY3NC0yNQ

Fixed: b:579925564
Test: SearchboxHandlerTest.*, CrComponentsComposeboxTest.*, NewTabPageTest.*Composebox, OmniboxPopupAimTest.*Composebox

IDB: make IDBIndex::Get implementation with keyrange faster, reuse code

Refactor Database::GetAllOperation and Database::GetOperation to be able
to share code. Where Database::GetOperation previously used Cursors,
i.e. in the keyrange case, now it uses the shared code.

Get (both ObjectStore and Index) with a KeyRange gets faster because the
Cursor already retrieves the value; the current code just used the
Cursor to get a key then re-queried the DB for the value. This was
especially silly for key-only queries which don't need a value!

Potential improvements or simplifications in the non-keyrange case are
left as a TODO pending better perf test coverage.

8bb127469e2a79be386f0b13d8079b156289afce [ diff ] 
[PDF V2] Separate toolbar edit mode from form filling in PDF viewer.

Typing into a PDF form field previously triggered an internal edit mode
journey in AndroidX. This caused the viewer to enter edit mode,
highlighting the edit button in the toolbar and displaying the "Done"
action without explicit user intent. Additionally, transitioning into
annotation edit mode could cause draft form edits to be discarded.

To address this, differentiate toolbar-initiated annotation mode from
form filling:
- Deregister the fragment's form edit listener so typing updates form
  content without activating the toolbar edit mode UI.
- Track toolbar edit mode with a dedicated state flag and restore it
  across configuration changes.
- Disable form filling while toolbar edit mode is active and re-enable
  it upon exit.
- Transition cleanly into edit mode without clearing active draft
  changes, ensuring both form entries and drawing annotations persist.

Verification: http://screencast/cast/NTY0ODMzNzAwODA2NjU2MHxmZTNhZDMwYi04NQ

d2df8856d1773d47f0fe4836f67652b0cbfb19be
parent
5f42040ef58ab757fa610eaba1b2f9c594fb1182 [ diff ] 
Invalidate subtree paint when effect reference filters change in place

Cross-origin frames, plugins, and view-transition content layers strip
ancestor SVG reference filters either during PrePaint
(SetupContextForFrame) or when recording foreign layers during Paint
(EmbeddedContentPainter::RemoveSvgFilterPaint and
EmbeddedContentPainter::EffectForViewTransitionContent).

Track HasReferenceFilter() changes across ObjectPaintProperties effect
nodes in FragmentPaintPropertyTreeBuilder::UpdateForSelf and
PaintPropertyTreeBuilder::InitPaintProperties, and force both an
isolation-piercing property update and full subtree paint invalidation
so stacked descendants re-record with the updated effect chain.

R=pdr@chromium.org, szager@chromium.org

glic: Emit DataTransferActionsBlockedNotice on blocked inputs

Non-DLP paste and drag-and-drop blocks into Glic currently fail
silently without UI feedback. Add an optional
GlicBrowserHost.getDataTransferActionsBlockedNotices() Observable
stream backed by WebClient.NotifyDataTransferActionsBlocked in
glic.mojom so the Glic web client can show blocked-input notices.

- Emit PASTE notices in glic::IsClipboardPasteAllowed for
  kPageContextIneligible, kCrossProfile, and kPageContextInvalidated
  (including non-tab WebContents), coalescing multi-format clipboard
  read bursts within 500ms per (InstanceId, seqno); eligible-tab pastes
  pass through to enterprise DataControls without emitting a notice.
- Emit DROP notices in glic::StartDragAndDropInvoke for blocked
  ineligible, cross-profile, unknown-source, and destroyed-frame drops.
- Forward notices synchronously to the primary WebClient if attached;
  drops or pastes onto uninitialized or loading clients do not queue.
- Expose the notice stream via GlicBrowserHost for web client
  subscriptions.

TAG=agy
CONV=804498f0-fc56-4f71-b6a4-78c3191fdf9c

[iOS] Avoid preparing tab switcher on App Bar touch down inside Tab Grid

When the Tab Grid is already visible, the App Bar's Tab Grid button
exits the Tab Grid on tap rather than opening it. Skip donating the open
Tab Grid intent and preparing the tab switcher on touch down when the
Tab Grid is already visible to avoid redundant snapshot generation.

0bb576d21aa7efa6f77b8dcf76f5804d4f40b015
parent
3f72e348f74f39a7b810bba5576eb5d3debf8fc8 [ diff ] 
Remove reinterpret_cast_ptr and unsafe_reinterpret_cast_ptr.

These helpers were originally added to work around pointer alignment
warnings on ARM with GCC. Since they are no longer needed, (chromium
does not compile with cast-align) this change removes them from
std_lib_extras.h and replaces their call sites across Blink with
standard reinterpret_cast. The NO_SANITIZE_UNRELATED_CAST attribute from
unsafe_reinterpret_cast_ptr is moved directly onto the Vector inline
buffer accessors.

Cq-Exclude-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:dawn-linux-x64-deps-rel,dawn-win10-x64-deps-rel

[omnibox] Hide native focus ring once Full WebUI popup paints

Problem: Clicking the page while the Full WebUI omnibox dropdown is open
flashes the native focus ring for 1 to 4 frames as the popup hides, and
Tab to the AI mode button leaves the native ring's outer 1px showing.
Since crrev.com/c/8574561, LocationBarView keeps its native ring under
kFull to cover popup frames that are see-through before the WebUI
paints.

Solution: Keep the native focus ring only until the popup's WebUI
completes its first visually non-empty paint, which Blink reports once
that frame is presented [1]. The popup keeps its WebContents across
shows [2], so from then on the WebUI's CSS ring is the only ring. Each
window's popup has its own WebContents, so a new window gets the native
ring again until its popup paints. Blink drops box-shadow in forced
colors mode, so the WebUI draws that ring as an outline there.

Reopens no longer get the native fill-in. Hiding the popup detaches its
contents [3], so a reopen can start see-through until the WebUI's next
frame, showing the location bar's focused look without a ring.

Forced-colors mode (`@media (forced-colors: active)`, focused &
collapsed):
- Before: http://screen/p2ztWtpXQoxy2yk
- After: http://screen/hnKDPSEY9cMj8gG

Alternatives considered:
1. Turning the native ring off for all of kFull drops it on new windows
   while the popup is see-through, which crrev.com/c/8574561 fixed.
2. Gating it on the dropdown (patchsets 1-4) misses the AI mode button
   case, which happens with the dropdown collapsed.
3. Re-arming it on every show needs proof that the WebUI's frame is on
   screen. The popup window's first frame isn't proof, since hiding
   detaches the contents [3], so crrev.com/c/8687885 can drop the ring
   early. Waiting for the WebUI's frame (patchsets 5-15) needs a forced
   redraw [4]. An idle Mac popup compositor submits no frame without
   damage, so the presentation callback never fires, and ScheduleDraw()
   only requests a commit. Patchset 16 used the visual-state callback
   that Show() requests instead. That frame can predate the focus state
   [5], and the presenter has to track per-show state.
4. A stand-in ring in FullWebUIOmniboxFrame [6] duplicates
   LocationBarView's ring geometry and must track in-page focus [7].

[1] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/2845b27bafc8ff4cbc0a101c71c8847de57a2164:third_party/blink/renderer/core/frame/web_frame_widget_impl.cc;l=3937-3957
[2] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/2845b27bafc8ff4cbc0a101c71c8847de57a2164:chrome/browser/ui/views/omnibox/omnibox_popup_presenter_base.cc;l=334-338
[3] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/2845b27bafc8ff4cbc0a101c71c8847de57a2164:chrome/browser/ui/views/omnibox/omnibox_popup_webui_base_content.cc;l=567-576
[4] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/2845b27bafc8ff4cbc0a101c71c8847de57a2164:ui/compositor/compositor.cc;l=451-458
[5] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/2845b27bafc8ff4cbc0a101c71c8847de57a2164:chrome/browser/ui/views/omnibox/omnibox_popup_view_full_webui.cc;l=496-507
[6] https://crrev.com/c/8692787
[7] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/2845b27bafc8ff4cbc0a101c71c8847de57a2164:chrome/browser/ui/views/omnibox/omnibox_full_popup_webui_content.cc;l=120-128

[OTP] Move OTP matching into GmailOtpService

Previously, GmailOtpRetriever queried GmailOtpBackend directly and
performed sender domain matching and subscription management itself,
requiring each caller to construct a DomainRelationChecker and pass
backend and affiliation dependencies. This CL moves cached OTP
filtering, incoming OTP subscription management, and sender domain
matching into GmailOtpService, simplifying GmailOtpRetriever to
coordinate between cached and live OTP candidates provided by the
service.

324f5d0330cef4bf4912f7800315657a4475ddec [ diff ] 
[iOS] Remove `webStateDidStopLoading` from WebStateDeferredExecutor

WebStateDeferredExecutor worked under the assumption that
didLoadPageWithSuccess fires on a successful load, and that
webStateDidStopLoading only triggers if a load was aborted without
completing.

In reality, the two callbacks come from different WKWebView mechanisms.
DidStopLoading would fire first and can also fire mid-navigation.
WKWebView can also toggle load from NO -> YES transiently during
provisional navigation and asynchronous policy decisions.

In this case `webState:didLoadPageWithSuccess:` should be enough to
cover both the failure and the success states based on the success
parameter.

🌺 Revert "[Download] Allow Finch to configure Android download features"

This reverts commit 4852a3a1e96992b35941e000cfdec8281008f75e.
Culprit: 4852a3a1e969 - "[Download] Allow Finch to configure Android download features" (refs/heads/main@{#1733980}, https://crrev.com/c/8678602)

Reason for revert: consistently fails
android-desktop-17-x64-rel-emu-tests.

Mechanism: Commit 4852a3a1e96992b35941e000cfdec8281008f75e changed
kEnableDownloadSaveAsContextMenu from FEATURE_ENABLED_BY_DEFAULT to
FEATURE_DISABLED_BY_DEFAULT and updated its call sites in
ChromeContextMenuItem.isSaveAsEnabled(),
IsDownloadSaveAsContextMenuEnabled(), and
JNI_OfflinePageDownloadBridge_StartDownload() from
isEnabled(kEnableDownloadSaveAsContextMenu) && isDesktop() to
isEnabled(kEnableDownloadSaveAsContextMenu) || isDesktop(). On Desktop
Android builders (DeviceInfo.isDesktop() == true), the Save-As path now
remains unconditionally active even when tests explicitly disable
kEnableDownloadSaveAsContextMenu via @DisableFeatures or
ScopedFeatureList: (1)
ChromeContextMenuPopulatorTest#testVideoDownloadVideoFrame (and 3
siblings) gets 'Save video frame as…' instead of 'Download video frame';
(2) ChromeDownloadManagerDelegateTest.RequestConfirmation_Android opens
the location dialog (CANCELED) instead of NO_DIALOG
(CONTINUE_WITHOUT_CONFIRMATION); and (3)
OfflinePageBridgeTest#testDownloadPage takes the || is_desktop() early
return (OnSavePage) instead of saving an offline page via
OfflinePageModel.

org.chromium.chrome.browser.contextmenu.ChromeContextMenuPopulatorTest#testVideoDownloadVideoFrame
has failed in 2/25 recent builds of
android-desktop-17-x64-rel-emu-tests, every attempt in every one of
them. This is not flake: it reproduces on every run, so it blocks the CQ
for every unrelated CL that touches this suite. The regression appears
at refs/heads/main@{#1733980}.

Example failing run:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chrome/builders/ci/android-desktop-17-x64-rel-emu-tests/6195

To run/deflake this test on CQ before relanding, add the following
footer to your CL description:
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chrome.android.desktop:android-desktop-17-x64-rel-emu-tests,chromium.android.desktop:android-desktop-x64-rel-15-tests|chrome_public_unit_test_apk

Reverting to green the tree first. Please re-land once the failure is
understood and covered; this revert is about restoring CQ throughput,
not about the merit of the change.

Original change's description:
> [Download] Allow Finch to configure Android download features
>
> Recently, Android download improvement features were enabled by default
> and gated behind a strict isDesktop(). This CL allow us to start those
> experiment features on non-desktop Android form factors (Phones,
> Tablets, and Foldables).
>
> Disable the following four features by default in their declarations and
> update their call sites to check isEnabled() || isDesktop() so Desktop
> Android keeps them enabled while non-desktop Android can be controlled
> via Finch:
> - kOpenDownloadInPreferredApp
> - kOpenDownloadInFilesAppIfNoHandlerFound
> - kOpenDownloadInNewTab
> - kEnableDownloadSaveAsContextMenu
>
> Bug: 521890621
> Change-Id: I31dc32e0f513b76c09158cd166da5a0b46dbe829
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8678602
> Commit-Queue: Min Qin <qinmin@chromium.org>
> Reviewed-by: David Trainor <dtrainor@chromium.org>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1733980}

[Signin] Use account preview data in ShowChromeSigninBubble if available

Populate BubbleParameters::account_preview_preference in
SigninViewController::ShowChromeSigninBubble() when
AccountPreviewDataService::GetPreferredAccountForSignin() returns a
preference matching the bubble's account. This ensures the Chrome Signin
bubble displays the custom account preview subtitle when triggered from
non-intercept entry points such as the 3-dots menu.

91224b5162a7475b36a4f61faca1627610d26c83
parent
36ba9a6d155b00c82e27d5ce9ba769ad2fbcc1f7 [ diff ] 
[blink] Have BucketFileSystemAgent own BucketFileSystemBuilder

Instead of allocating BucketFileSystemBuilder as a fire-and-forget
object kept alive via SelfKeepAlive<BucketFileSystemBuilder>, have
BucketFileSystemAgent own active builders in a
HeapHashSet<Member<BucketFileSystemBuilder>> until they complete or the
agent is disposed.

BucketFileSystemBuilder now also:
- Fails the DevTools callback and removes itself from the agent on Mojo
  disconnect or ExecutionContext destruction (fixing a leak where local
  HeapMojoReceiver reset on context destruction never cleared
  self_keep_alive_).
- Consumes completion_callback_ via std::exchange on completion so
  multiple failing AsBlob() calls cannot invoke it more than once.

TAG=agy
CONV=add4b94a-6201-4542-9861-434e43697a99

74fbe209b94a35150342d09f0a9b7d65846a73e3 [ diff ] 
Pass ptc=1 on supervised user pending-state reauth interstitials

Behind `ReauthSkipIdentifierPageFollowup`,
`SupervisedUserVerificationPage` sets `proceed_to_challenge = true`
(`ptc=1`) when building `reauth_url_` via
`signin::GetChromeReauthURL()`, so Gaia skips the identifier page when
re-authenticating a supervised user from YouTube or blocked-site
pending-state interstitials.

See demo in the bug entry.

[Facilitated Payments] Log the latency of QR code heuristic scans

Adds `FacilitatedPayments.QrCode.Heuristics.Latency`, which records in
microseconds how long `FacilitatedPaymentsAgent` takes to calculate the
heuristic signals of a payment QR code. The scan runs synchronously on
the renderer main thread, so this measures the cost that QR code
detection adds to the page.

tree
fcb4e0a3e9f37fa1aa3f9f5a562ada1e7356bfb3
parent
5760897b2648b61fdcbc39cd339a38de6504f9c3 [ diff ] 
content: Clear select popup anchor view immediately when dismissed

In crrev.com/c/8230048 we made sure popups and touch handles are
dismissed when a WebView detaches from its window so they don't survive
AwContents#adopt.

For <select> popups, hiding the popup dismissed the Java dialog right
away, but the native side waited for the renderer to close the Mojo
connection before removing the anchor view.

When reparenting a WebView, we detach the old view and adopt into the
new one synchronously, before that renderer round trip comes back. The
lingering anchor view then gets moved into the new WebView during
construction. This previously went unnoticed because adoption ran at the
end of the WebView constructor, but crrev.com/c/8151302 moved WebView
provider initialization earlier in the constructor hierarchy (before
ViewGroup is ready to accept child views), causing this to crash.

Clear the anchor view and Mojo client as soon as the popup finishes or
is cancelled on the browser side.

Bug: b:531633054
Test: SelectPopupTest#testHideRemovesAnchorView

[PDF Signing] Add signature ID types and PdfInkUndoRedoModel support

Introduce InkSignatureId and InkLoadedSignatureId in pdf_ink_ids.h to
identify newly created and pre-existing PDF signature annotations, along
with a SignatureId variant for representing both types.

Update PdfInkUndoRedoModel to support these signature ID types in the
same manner as InkTextId and InkLoadedTextId:
- Allow InkSignatureId in Commands::adds via IsAllowedInCommandsStack(),
  and require a prior Add() command before Remove().
- Allow InkLoadedSignatureId in Remove() without a prior Add() command.
- Add GetUndoSignatureId() and GetRedoInkSignatureId(), sharing their
  implementations with GetUndoTextId() and GetRedoInkTextId() via
  templated GetUndoId() and GetRedoId() helpers.

This CL is AI generated.

[smart selection] Don't select an area on click with the prompt box

With the prompt box on, a click on the overlay background no longer
selects an area around it. It does nothing while nothing is selected,
and closes the overlay once there is a region. Drags still add regions.

1173c862f28d05eea3a860a81ace86e1a419e3d2
parent
2172f68dd00b6afc440efc0261564f97a2c2af4a [ diff ] 
[scroll-snap] Don't treat unreachable snap areas as visible

SnapSearchResult::visible_range() returns a valid range even when the
snap area is entirely outside the snapport. Use RangeF::Intersect()
instead to exclude snap areas outside the snapport. Add
RangeF::ClosedRange() to represent a closed [min, max] range, so that
areas touching the edge are still considered visible.

Add WPT snap-to-visible-areas-unreachable.html, covering a snap area in
negative overflow, a snap area clipped past the scrollable overflow, and
zero-width snap areas at the start and end edges.
